🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)76:Te51,Closes #11776.

## Problem

The fused `eliza_inference_kokoro_synthesize()` takes **raw text** and phonemizes inside the lib. Real espeak-ng G2P is compiled in only under `KOKORO_USE_ESPEAK`; otherwise it uses a lossy per-byte ASCII grapheme fallback. Every fused build that does **not** link libespeak-ng — **Android + iOS always, desktop whenever the host lacks libespeak-ng dev files** — produced speech-shaped but **unintelligible** audio.

## Fix

The kokoro lib already had `ipa_to_token_ids()` (embedded 115-entry vocab, exact reference ids); it just wasn't reachable through the fused FFI. Expose it and let the TS layer feed espeak-ng IPA when the lib can't phonemize.

**Fork** (gitlink bump `dda200ab0` → `66ab678cb`, a descendant of the develop pin — elizaOS/llama.cpp#43 **merged**, additive ABI v12 → v14):
- `eliza_inference_kokoro_g2p_kind(ctx)` → `ESPEAK` | `ASCII` capability query.
- `eliza_inference_kokoro_synthesize_ipa(ctx, ipa, …)` → synthesize from precomputed espeak-ng IPA via `ipa_to_token_ids()`, bypassing the in-lib phonemizer.
- v13 (vision describe) is the main-lineage surface, so the develop-pinned lineage advances **12 → 14** for Kokoro IPA to stay collision-free through the #11386 fork reconciliation.

**TS runtime**:
- `ffi-bindings.ts`: bind the two new symbols as an additive kokoro-g2p family; add a cascade rung for the develop-pinned fork lineage (v12 + Kokoro IPA **without** the main-lineage vision-stream v13 symbols); accept a lib reporting v14; keep accepting v13/v12. `ELIZA_INFERENCE_ABI_VERSION` 13 → 14.
- `KokoroFfiRuntime` queries `g2p_kind` once at load and routes per lib: **espeak** → raw text (keeps the #11238 no-double-phonemization fix); **ascii** → feed the espeak-ng-WASM IPA the TS phonemizer already produced through `synthesize_ipa`; **unknown** (pre-v14 lib) → raw text with a loud one-time warning. Lossy dev-phonemizer → warn once, still use the IPA entry.

**Staging honesty**: `stage-desktop-fused-lib.mjs` warns loudly (non-fatal) when the host lacks libespeak-ng (the TS IPA path keeps it intelligible). Desktop + iOS verify-symbol lists require the two new v14 symbols.

## Evidence

Full bundle + WAVs in `.github/issue-evidence/11776-kokoro-ipa-g2p/`. All WER from real `eliza-1-asr` ASR round-trips I ran and read (no mock).

**Before vs after (same espeak-less lib):**

| G2P path | espeak-**less** lib | espeak-**linked** baseline |
|---|---|---|
| RAW-TEXT (old default — the bug) | **0.958** garbled | 0.042 |
| WASM-IPA (this fix) | **0.042** intelligible | 0.042 |

The fix reaches the espeak-linked baseline exactly.

- **Desktop real TS runtime** (`kokoro-real-smoke.ts`, espeak-less lib): WER **0.13**, `phonemizer=phonemizer`, envelope-cv 1.228.
- **Android emulator-5554** (arm64, real **76 MB** statically-fused `.so`, `g2p_kind=0/ASCII`): RAW-TEXT mean WER **0.958** → WASM-IPA mean WER **0.042**, non-empty accurate transcript ("The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog." verbatim) — the exact round-trip that returned an **empty** transcript in #10727's emu leg. Method: standalone NDK C harness dlopening the real fused `.so` and driving the real IPA entry with the espeak-ng-WASM IPA the TS layer produces.
- **Fork native tests** (macOS, espeak linked): `test_kokoro_phonemes` OK, `test_kokoro_g2p_espeak` ALL PASS (new g2p-kind + IPA-input-parity asserts).

`bun run typecheck` clean; kokoro + ffi-bindings suites: 82 passed / 2 skipped.

**iOS** inherits the fix (platform-neutral TS+FFI; `g2p=ascii`); on-device iOS capture tracked by #11612-residual / #11734.

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)77:T12c9,## Problem

`VIEWS create` / `APP create` (plugin-app-control, surface agent-chat-views) only work from a monorepo checkout:

- the min-plugin / min-project templates and the `plugins/` landing dir are resolved solely from `ELIZA_REPO_ROOT`/cwd (`views-create.ts` template candidates + plugins-dir candidates, same pattern in `app-create.ts`), so a packaged desktop install — or just a wrong cwd — always dead-ends with `min-plugin template not found (tried: ...)`;
- the flows silently depend on plugin-agent-orchestrator (`START_CODING_TASK`) and a logged-in coding CLI backend, discover that only AFTER scaffolding, and leave a half-created workdir plus an unactionable error (`Scaffolded X, but could not dispatch a coding agent: START_CODING_TASK action not registered`).

Repro on develop (`runViewsCreate` with `repoRoot` = empty dir, simulating a packaged install):

```
success: false
min-plugin template not found (tried: /tmp/packaged-install-NvHOle/packages/elizaos/templates/min-plugin,
/tmp/packaged-install-NvHOle/eliza/packages/elizaos/templates/min-plugin); cannot scaffold a new view plugin.
```

## Fix

New shared `src/actions/scaffold-env.ts` used by both `views-create.ts` and `app-create.ts`:

1. **Templates resolve from the installed `elizaos` package** when the repo-root candidates miss. The `elizaos` CLI package publishes `templates/` in its `files` array; it is now a declared dependency of plugin-app-control so packaged builds always ship the templates.
2. **New plugins land in `<stateDir>/plugins`** when the repo root has no `plugins/` dir (registered via load-from-directory like any external plugin dir, same as rollback re-registration).
3. **`preflightCodingDispatch` runs BEFORE any scaffolding** in create/edit paths: checks `START_CODING_TASK`/`CREATE_TASK` registration and probes PATH for a coding CLI (claude/codex/opencode). Failures answer with actionable setup guidance instead of a dead-end, and nothing is scaffolded. An explicit `ELIZA_ACP_DEFAULT_AGENT`/`ELIZA_ACP_CLI` config is trusted and skips the probe (managed/remote executors may not have the CLI on the server's PATH).
4. Template-missing errors now carry setup guidance (point `ELIZA_REPO_ROOT` at a checkout or install the `elizaos` package).

After (same packaged-install scenario, orchestrator absent):

```
I can't build a view plugin yet. The coding-agent orchestrator is not loaded (no START_CODING_TASK action).
Add @elizaos/plugin-agent-orchestrator to this agent's plugins to enable scaffolding.
```

and with orchestrator + CLI present, the full flow works from an empty repoRoot:

```
workdir: /tmp/eliza-state-8bQJmW/plugins/plugin-me-crypto-price-ticker   (under <stateDir>/plugins)
SCAFFOLD.md present: true, placeholders rewritten, dispatch prompt has sourceDir: true
Started view create task for Me Crypto Price Ticker at ...
```

## Tests

- `scaffold-env.test.ts` — template resolution (checkout + installed-package fallback), `<stateDir>/plugins` fallback, PATH probe (real temp executables), preflight matrix (missing orchestrator / missing CLI / both / explicitly configured).
- `views-create.packaged.test.ts` — drives the real `runViewsCreate` handler end to end from an empty repoRoot: scaffolds the real min-plugin template from the installed `elizaos` package into `<stateDir>/plugins` with placeholders rewritten and dispatch against that workdir; and proves preflight failure answers with guidance and scaffolds **nothing**.
- `views-management.test.ts` runtime stub declares a configured backend so the existing create-flow tests stay deterministic on hosts without a coding CLI.

## Verification

- `bun run --cwd plugins/plugin-app-control test` — 1302 passed, 1 failed: `verification-room-bridge.test.ts` boot-order retry, **pre-existing** (fails identically on pristine develop, verified via stash).
- `bun run --cwd plugins/plugin-app-control typecheck` — clean.
- `bun run --cwd plugins/plugin-app-control build` — clean (tsup + tsc declarations + views bundle).
- `lint:check` fails on pristine develop for this plugin (tabs committed vs space biome config, repo-wide drift) — new/edited code matches the committed tab style; no new lint classes introduced.

## Notes

- `packages/core/src/features/plugin-manager/actions/plugin-handlers/create.ts` has the same repo-root-only template resolution; left untouched here (separate surface) — can follow up if wanted.
- Evidence note: this is a CLI/runtime flow with no UI change; artifacts above are the real handler transcripts and real scaffolded files (no mocked template/filesystem). Live end-to-end with a real coding agent requires a logged-in CLI + running orchestrator and is the demo path for the app+cloud flow session.

*   Boosted mobile performance by lazy-loading heavy components and implementing RAM-class inference memory policies ([elizaos/eliza#11874](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11874), [elizaos/eliza#11822](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11822)).

**Cloud Infrastructure & Monetization**
*   Hardened monetization security by gating revenue on review status and preventing backup restore bypasses ([elizaos/eliza#11872](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11872), [elizaos/eliza#11843](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11843), [elizaos/eliza#11828](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11828)).
*   Expanded advertising infrastructure with support for new providers (LinkedIn, Reddit, X, Snap) and added campaign dayparting and performance reporting ([elizaos/eliza#11769](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11769), [elizaos/eliza#11703](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11703), [elizaos/eliza#11681](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11681), [elizaos/eliza#11740](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11740)).
*   Added org-scoped file management and audio/SFX generation providers ([elizaos/eliza#11758](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11758), [elizaos/eliza#11765](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11765)).

**Model Training & Optimization**
*   Implemented a judge-graded GEPA scoring lane for LifeOps capabilities ([elizaos/eliza#11901](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11901)).
*   Deployed an end-to-end fine-tuning pipeline for `eliza-1-2b` using `gpt-5.5` trajectories ([elizaos/eliza#11891](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11891)).
*   Integrated hosted Gemma-4 MTP drafters to improve local inference performance ([elizaos/eliza#11782](https://github.com/elizaos/eliza/pull/11782)).
